#6788. REI Is Closed for Black Friday, but It Still Has Deals on Yeti, Garmin, and Arc’teryx

The brand’s deals and Outlet sections have up to 50% off some top gear.

Our product picks are editor-tested, expert-approved. We may earn a commission through links on our site. Why Trust Us?

EVERY HOLIDAY SEASON for the past decade, REI has shut its doors and ceased online order processing on Black Friday. Instead of holding a Black Friday sale, the outdoor retailer encourages customers (and its employees) to #OptOutside by getting in nature and on an adventure. This Black Friday marks 11 years of that. But, the good thing for you and your holiday shopping is that REI still has a robust deals section, which currently has up to 50% off top brands.

I combed through and found top-tested picks, some year-after-year stalwarts, and some rare savings you should scoop up. Do keep in mind that REI does not process online orders on Black Friday either. And no word yet on Cyber Week savings from the retailer, but we’ll be sure to update this post when there’s details on that.

Smartwool Classic All-Season Merino Long-Sleeve

An easy deal to scoop up around the holidays is Yeti drinkware—makes for a great gift, and you probably have a mug around the house or office that needs replacing as well. This Rambler Tumbler (in the ridgeline color shown) is 20 percent off and will get the job done this winter. And there’s a super rare deal on the Yeti Tundra 45 Hard Cooler in two colors right now: 20% off the ridgeline color (shown above) and 30% off the pace purple color.

When it comes to trail runners, two of my favorites in recent times have been the Keen Seek and the Arc’teryx Norvan LD4. The Seek is Keen’s very first trail running shoe, and it was a surprise gem of 2025. The upper collar provides a comfortable, secure fit and the midsole foam and less aggressive lugs make it a nice gravel option. I’ve still got my original pair in the rotation after roughly 115 miles on them—29 percent off is a solid price. And the Norvan LD4 is another one of my favorites from 2025. Very stable and a tongue/collar that’s one of the most comfortable sock-like fits I’ve worn—feels like it’s just a thin bit of material and nothing more.

A few other standout deals right now are the Danner Mountain 600 Leaf GTX (our best hiking boots) discounted 20 percent, $100 off the Garmin Instinct 3 Solar, and just $90 for the Gregory Arrio 24 Pack (a longtime MH editor daypack favorite). Dig around and find that next piece of outdoor gear for a steal!

No, REI does not have a Black Friday sale in 2026. The outdoor retailer will close all 190-plus store locations (while giving all employees a paid day) and encourages customers and members to #OptOutside for the day. REI did run its Holiday Sale from November 13 to 23 in which customers could score up to 60 percent off outdoor gear.

While those specific savings are over, there is still the brand’s deals and Outlet sections offering discounts. No word yet on whether there will be any Cyber Week savings next week (stay tuned as we get closer).

Do also keep in mind that REI will not process online orders on Black Friday either.

Almost everyone who loves the outdoors knows that REI is ground zero for gearing up for hiking, biking, kayaking, climbing, fishing—pretty much any type of adventure, really. REI Outlet is the retailer's dedicated discount section, a roundup of last season's gear and its famous flash sales. It's the single best place to shop if you're looking for the absolute best deals on the world's best outdoor brands. You'll find great deals year-round, but the deepest discounts are almost always during REI's holiday sale events. Don't forget to check back early and often as REI Outlet's inventory changes frequently. Sale prices can drop suddenly and things sell out fast!

REI offers many of its best perks and sale event access to REI Co-op members. Even if you're not a frequent REI shopper, it's worth the one-time $30 fee to join. REI members also receive a 10% dividend on most in-store and online purchases, as well as receiving discounts on gear rentals and during REI's best annual sale events (like the holiday sale). That $30 can easily pay for itself in just one visit!

He has written about the outdoors for nearly two decades and was previously the Managing Editor, Content Performance at Gear Patrol. He holds a B.A. in Sport Management and is a Certified Beer Server, having previously covered the craft beer industry. Ryan currently lives on the water in New Jersey with his wife and their dog.

Finding stocking stuffers for men is low-key the biggest challenge of the holiday season. Somehow every year—without fail—it's always the cousins/brothers-in-law/teenage brothers who end up being the hardest to find gifts for. It has to be small enough to fit in what is essentially a decorative sock, cool enough that he won't immediately regift it, and useful enough that it doesn't end up in the abyss of his nightstand drawer. No pressure!

I've done the hard part for you and rounded up the best stocking stuffers for men that are actually worth buying—think tech accessories, grooming upgrades, workout essentials, and a few wildcard finds that just make sense. Whether you're shopping for your boyfriend, dad, teenage brother, or that one guy friend who's impossible to buy for, there's something here that'll get a genuine reaction instead of a polite nod. Consider this your permission to quit the 2 A.M. Amazon doomscroll.

If you want your gifts to arrive by Christmas, you’ll need to plan ahead—especially with holiday shipping volume peaking in December. For USPS, the recommended ship-by dates this year are Dec. 17 for Ground Advantage and First-Class Mail, Dec. 18 for Priority Mail, and Dec. 20 for Priority Mail Express. FedEx and UPS have similar mid-December deadlines that get more expensive the closer you get to Christmas Day. Some mega-retailers like Amazon offer two-day delivery or overnight shipping depending on what you're ordering and the delivery location. The short answer? Order soon and always check the estimated delivery date before you buy.

#6783. Glitter Makeup Is Taking Over Party Season—These Picks Make It Wearable

From subtle shimmer to disco-ball glam, these formulas bring the sparkle.

Every item on this page was chosen by an ELLE editor. We may earn commission on some of the items you choose to buy.

‘Tis the season to sparkle, and that includes makeup too. Glitter eyeshadow? Check. A sparkly lipstick? Done! Shimmering cheeks? You got it! Glitter makeup is currently taking over the holiday season and we’re here for it.

Gone are the days when glitter meant a chunky, craft-store mess that followed you home from the party (and showed up in your bed a week later). Today’s formulas are finer, smoother, and far more wearable, with ultra-fine particles, gel and liquid bases that grip the skin, and pressed pigments that deliver a high-shine finish without the fallout. Whether you’re dressing up for an office party, a New Year’s Eve countdown, or simply want your everyday look to catch the twinkle lights, there’s a glittery option made for you.

Below, we’ve rounded up the best glitter makeup, from an easy-to-apply wash of shimmer on the lids to full-on disco-ball glam. Consider this your shortlist for holiday party season.

Bring on the ethereal shine with Dior’s Glazed Violet lipstick. Available in two dazzling shades, it adds a touch of sparkle to your look without going overboard.

Half Magic's glitter pots are highly rated for their showstopping sparkle. Dubbed "grown-up" glitter, they deliver high-impact shine in a refined, wearable formula that's easy to apply and built to last through the party.

With a high percentage of reflective pearls, this baked highlighter is a silky, buildable formula that melts into the skin for a luminous, lit-from-within glow. Sweep it on lightly for a soft, dewy sheen, or layer it up for a high-impact, party-ready shine on the cheekbones, brow bones, and Cupid’s bow.

The glitter body gel from the early aughts, redefined for this new era of sparkle. With a blend of multidimensional, multicolored glitters, this mess-free gel is ready to be applied anywhere on your face or body, whether you’re dabbing it on the lids, tracing it along your collarbones, or going all-out from head to toe.

#6781. Is It Rude to Ask Guests to Take Their Shoes Off at Your House?

Your party, your rules? Etiquette experts say holiday hosting calls for a little more nuance.

Every item on this page was chosen by an ELLE Decor editor. We may earn commission on some of the items you choose to buy.

You’ve asked guests to dress for dinner. Is it fair to ask them to take part of the outfit off at the door? It’s a small holiday-hosting dilemma with reasonable arguments on either side: The host would rather not have wet boots on the good rug; the guest would rather not spend a cocktail party in stocking feet.

So, is the request rude? Not inherently, according to the etiquette and entertaining experts we asked. But the occasion, the advance notice, and the room you leave for exceptions all matter. Here’s how to handle it before the doorbell rings.

For some households, taking shoes off is simply what you do when you come in, whether you’re stopping by for coffee or staying for Thanksgiving dinner. There’s a practical argument for keeping the rule during the holidays, too. Stacey Gustafson, Bona’s flooring and cleaning expert, points out that grit tracked in on outdoor shoes can scratch hardwood and wear down its finish over time.

But not every shoes-off request is a bid to preserve perfect floors. Removing outdoor footwear is an established cultural custom in Japan, for example, where the genkan, or entrance area, separates outside from in. What one guest considers an unexpected inconvenience may be an entirely ordinary expression of respect in another person’s home.

For David Jubé, founder of food and entertaining platform The Gourmet Host, the request itself is less of a problem than the way it’s delivered. “Asking guests to take their shoes off is not rude. Making them guess is,” he says. “The rudeness, when it happens, comes from springing the request at the door with no warning, so a guest finds out in front of everyone that they wore mismatched socks or that their boots take five minutes to unlace.”

“If you’ve invited guests to dress up for an elegant holiday dinner, asking them to remove their shoes upon arrival can undermine the very experience you’ve invited them to enjoy,” says Erika Gessin, founder and creative director of entertaining brand The Fête Edit. “Shoes are often an intentional part of an outfit.”

If shoes are coming off regardless, guests should know before they plan what to wear. For hosts willing to relax their usual rule, etiquette expert Nick Leighton suggests letting the occasion take precedence: “For larger or more formal gatherings, it’s ideal to just let everyone keep their shoes on and treat the cleaning you’ll have to do afterwards as part of the cost of entertaining.”

Comfort matters as much as appearance. Amber Dawn Schultheis, creator of hosting and recipe site The Host Notes, points out that some guests need the support of their shoes, especially if they’ll be standing for an extended period. Her preferred approach leaves the decision with them: “Shoes are optional. Do what makes you feel good.”

If you’re keeping the rule, make it easy to follow. Jubé suggests writing, “We’re a shoes-off house, so bring cozy socks or slippers if you like” on the invitation. At the door, give guests somewhere to sit while they unlace their boots, a rack or tray to put them on, and a basket of clean slippers or grippy socks. If someone needs to keep their shoes on for health, mobility, or safety reasons, let them do so without asking for an explanation.

And if you’re the guest? Take your cue from the shoes already by the door, not from what you do at home. If it isn’t clear, Gessin recommends asking quietly: “Would you prefer I take my shoes off?”

Jubé recommends a sturdy mat outside, a washable one inside, and a boot tray for wet footwear. Gessin suggests washable runners in high-traffic areas—the hall between the front door and the dining room is a good place to start. Those small adjustments let you give guests a choice without spending the party keeping tabs on the floor.

Once everyone is inside, resist the urge to monitor every footprint. “Guests remember how a gathering felt, not whether the floor stayed spotless, and a host fretting over every footprint makes everyone tense,” Jubé says.

“Floors can be cleaned tomorrow,” Gessin says. “How you made your guests feel is what they’ll remember.”

#6772. Denmark’s Queen Mary Delivers a Trifecta of Designer Looks During Her Historic State Visit to Colombia

From a D&G pencil skirt to a Saloni maxi dress

Denmark’s Queen Mary is looking posh and joyful on day two of her historic state visit to Colombia—a trip which makes her the first Danish queen to travel to the country.

Today, the monarch was given a tour of the Gold Museum in Bogotá, and later walked through the city’s main square, Plaza de Bolívar, with Mayor Carlos Fernando Galán.

The official visit runs through November 26, and the queen’s agenda includes learning about the country’s post-earthquake reconstruction efforts (three months after a 7.4-magnitude earthquake struck Colombia) and ongoing sustainability projects.

To arrive in the country, Mary went sharp in a black Forte Forte single-breasted blazer with a frilly sky-blue Stenströms Darya blouse, which she paired with black trousers, Malone Souliers Marion 70 pumps, and black Gucci glasses, plus some gold jewelry.

Then, for this afternoon’s engagements, she slipped into a sophisticated business look, the highlight of which was a black-and-white tweed pencil skirt from Dolce & Gabbana and a black collared top from Red Valentino. To round out the ensemble, she reached for sheer black tights, plus matching stilettos and a leather handbag.

The royal performed another quick change for her evening engagement: a reception in the Biodiversario greenhouse at Bogotá’s Botanical Garden. The look was a breezy wine midi dress with a fun dotted print by Saloni. In semi-sheer fabric, it featured a high neck, long sleeves, a cinched waist, and a long skirt that reached her ankles. Black-and-white heels added the perfect contrast to the colorful maxi.

#6770. The 8 Best Sweatpants for Men, Tested and Reviewed by Style Editors

Our product picks are editor-tested, expert-approved. We may earn a commission through links on our site. Why Trust Us?

Lululemon and Vuori were the top winners in our testing.

BACK IN MY college days at Howard University, sweatpants were basically my uniform. I wore them to class, the dining hall, the computer lab, and pretty much anywhere I could get away with it. They weren't about style back then; they were comfort clothes, plain and simple. But somewhere between campus life and my current life as a style editor, menswear brands reinvented casual sweatpants, breaking out of the shell of simple loungewear or workout clothes. The best ones now have structure, shape, and a sense of polish. They're made from soft, breathable fabrics like cotton, fleece, or jersey, but they fit better, move better, and actually look like you tried.

We tested over 35 different styles to bring you the absolute best. We’ve got options blended with reasonable stretch, performance tech, and even a few jogger hybrids. Our list covers top names like Alo and Abercrombie, and gives you the lowdown on styles like Vuori vs. Lululemon with each one bringing something different to the table.

Since my college days, I’ve tested countless sweats, but Luluemon’s ABC Jogger takes the cake. From its clean aesthetic and flexible wear for multiple settings to overall comfort and performance, I’ve worn this pair into the ground. Thanks to the synthetic, workout-friendly fabrics that wick away moisture, this sweatpant from Lululemon lets me breathe freely in the deepest sense. During my trial run, I wore it for some intense, sweat-dripping workouts, but the fabric is so lightweight that I barely felt it on my body. Even after hours of wear, this pair stayed fresh and dry, proving its versatility for tough sessions and casual lounging.

It’s the rare sweatpant that’s comfy enough for the couch but sharp enough to go get your pick-me-up of the day from the local coffee shop. Not to mention, the material is infused with four-way stretch to move with you through everything. As for the name, ABC stands for anti-ball crushing, which means the pants allow plenty of breathing space to keep your crotch area cool.

In terms of construction, the pant has a classic tapered look that keeps things polished. For a little added functionality, the pants have a hidden zipper back pocket to hold bills or keys as you’re commuting to your next destination.

Champion’s fleece joggers are the best mashup of a traditional sweatpant and a jogger. They’re made from a midweight fleece that feels solid enough for fall and winter, but the tapered leg and ankle cuffs give them a cleaner shape. The inside is brushed to a ridiculously soft, almost teddy-bear-like finish, so these are the pants I’d reach for when the temperature drops and I want something warm without feeling weighed down.

I wouldn’t necessarily reserve them for workouts, considering how thick they feel. But I’ve worn them for cool morning walks or days when I just want to get out of the house and move my body. The cotton-polyester blend has enough stretch to keep the tailored fit from feeling restrictive, even if you have bigger thighs. After multiple washes, they’ve held up nicely without quickly pilling. For $34, they’re also surprisingly affordable for how durable they feel. I’d just air-dry them to help keep that soft fleece feeling intact.

Nike’s dominance in activewear is undeniable, but its lineup extends to pieces that go beyond the gym. For instance, I wear this pair of sweatpants for an early-morning flight, a quick trip to pick up my dry cleaning or pretty much any day when comfort takes priority but I still want to look presentable. The inside is my favorite part, though. It’s brushed for a soft finish, making these especially good for cooler temperatures. They’re made from a cotton-polyester blend and have a standard fit that gives you room through the seat and thighs without going full baggy.

There’s no stretch woven into the fabric, but I didn’t find that to be much of an issue sitting in my cramped airplane seat. These are better suited for sport-casual days than anything involving serious movement. The open hem also gives them a cleaner, more traditional sweatpant shape rather than the tapered look of a jogger. And with sizing that goes up to 4XL, there’s a much broader range of guys who can find a comfortable fit.

Whether you’re jogging, low-impact training, or shooting hoops, Vuori’s performance pant is designed for light to moderate activities. The proprietary DreamKnit fabric is buttery soft and light, so you stay comfortable during movement. During yoga, we found that the added stretch was ideal for dynamic movements like deep lunges, twists, and stretches, where flexibility and range of motion are essential. Similar to our favorite workout shirt, its moisture-wicking properties kept us dry, preventing the pants from clinging to the body even during sweatier sessions.

True to its laid-back SoCal roots, Vuori made the fit to be relaxed and breathable, ensuring you stay cool. The tapered leg provides a modern silhouette without hugging too tightly, meaning airflow is never compromised, making these ideal for everything from exercise warm-ups to cooldowns. Beyond its performance specs, these pants simply look great on. Our editors reach for this pair to tackle last-minute errands like dry cleaning drop-offs and grocery store runs. We also appreciate the thoughtful zippered pocket on the leg—perfect for securely storing your phone or other valuables that don’t add bulk and cramp your style.

Matching Sweatshirt: Ponto Full Zip Hoodie

Relaxed fit sweatpants offer more room, better comfort. Take this pair from Reigning Champ. They offer plenty of room without looking excessively oversized. Since it's a loose fit with extra volume, I could feel the extra airflow coming up my leg, so I didn't overheat when I was lounging around like a couch potato. And the midweight terry fabric is dense but breathable, meaning it's got some heft and keeps you warm in 50-degree weather.

Unlike slim-fit sweats that hug your legs, this cut feels looser and has a straight drape that makes you look put-together and not sloppy. You don't see this often: These sweatpants have drawcord cuffs, which let you cinch them for a more tapered, jogger-like aesthetic. Wear them loose for a laid-back feel or snug for a sportier, more streamlined silhouette.

Matching Sweatshirt: Reigning Champ Midweight Terry Standard Hoodie

The whole point of sweatpants is comfort, and Uniqlo’s nailed it. For starters, the wide, straight-leg cut gives your legs and thighs ample breathing room, so you're never feeling clammy. The inside has a really smooth surface that feels cozy against the skin. The outside of the pants is equally smooth to the touch and has a polished look that makes them versatile enough for casual outings where the vibes are laid-back and not too serious. We’ve gotten plenty of use out of them for casual brunches and outdoor festivals. In addition, the elastic waistband gives you a snug, custom fit without the dreaded sag.

As the most affordable pair out of the tested options, these still measure up in quality. They’ll bring warmth in temperatures in the high 50s to low 60s, which is perfect for city commuters and everyday gym-goers. Another bonus: The front and back pockets give plenty of room to stash your essentials without worrying about them falling out.

Matching Sweatshirt: Uniqlo Sweat Oversized Pullover Hoodie

If you’re looking for something that can move from lounging to work to travel, feast your eyes on these street-ready sweatpants from Alo. Your favorite college sweats received a revamp with premium French terry on the outside and warm fleece on the interior. In fact, we found the structured fleece feels exceptionally smooth and cozy as soon as you slide the pants on, and the fit is perfect for a relaxing evening on the sofa. Also, the fabric is much thicker than others on the list, making it suitable for colder temps from winter through spring.

The straight leg keeps things polished, so you won't look like you just rolled out of bed. We styled these sweatpants with everything from a matching crewneck and hoodie for a full-on sweatsuit look. Unlike the standard relaxed cuts we’ve put through the wringer, this one is designed with an oversized fit, but doesn’t make you appear slouchy with excessive fabric.

Abercrombie makes some of the most stylish sweatpants I’ve tested, which is why I’ll be wearing them for the rest of fall. I predict that relaxed, straight-leg pants will be one of the upcoming season’s biggest trends, and this pair fits the bill. The silhouette is roomy but still looks clean enough that I don’t feel like I’m wearing straight-up pajama pants. It’s a great option for guys who don’t skip leg day.

The super-soft fleece is comfortable without being overly bulky. Instead of cinching around your ankle like a traditional jogger, the leg falls straight down and lightly drapes over your sneakers. I like the way it sits over my Asics without creating a bunch of fabric stacking around the shoe. Wear them with a T-shirt and sneakers, or go for the full matching set with Abercrombie’s coordinating hoodie. There are also plenty of colors to choose from, so you can build a set or mix them into the rest of your wardrobe.

Look for soft, breathable materials like cotton, fleece, or jersey that keep you cozy but still allow for movement. Some performance fabrics wick moisture away and provide added durability.

Your best bet will be to find options with a waistband that won’t dig in and enough room in the thighs. Different styles offer different looks and levels of comfort, and understanding the options can help you select the right pair for your needs. Here’s a breakdown of the most common sweatpant fits.

The relaxed fit is all about breathability and unlimited mobility. These sweatpants offer extra room around the hips and thighs, providing a looser feel throughout the seat. It's ideal for lounging, casual wear, and people who prioritize comfort over a tailored look. Think of it as the ultimate chill-out pant—easygoing and roomy, perfect for a lazy day, lying around the house.

Tapered sweatpants are looser at the top (around the hips and thighs) but gradually get narrower toward the ankle. This creates a more streamlined, modern silhouette for active people who want to move easily while maintaining a fashionable appearance. These pants give off a sportier vibe, perfect for gym sessions, running errands, or looking good while staying comfortable.

Slim fit sweatpants are more form-fitting and cut closer to the body, especially in the thighs and calves. They offer a sleek, minimalist look without being as tight as leggings. They’re ideal for everyday wear and even casual work settings when paired with an appropriate top.

Joggers are a type of tapered sweatpant that features a cuff at the ankle. They often have a more athletic, streetwear-inspired look and are typically made from a thinner material. The hybrid look is favored among athleisure lovers and those who like a sporty, on-the-go aesthetic. Joggers are suitable for running, light workouts, or even running errands.

Features like hidden zippered pockets enhance functionality, especially for travel. Deep side pockets can be super handy for carrying your phone, keys, wallet, earbuds, or small bills while on the go. And if you’re being a couch potato for the weekend, you can stash your remote control or snack in there as well.

Our style team, led by Style Director Christian Gollayan, put each pair through real-life scenarios: casual hangouts, city commutes, travel days, and of course, workout sessions. To keep things fair, we created a rubric and scored them across four key categories: comfort, durability, fit, and versatility.

We assessed how soft and breathable they felt, how well they survived workouts and laundry days, whether they struck the right balance between relaxed and polished, and how easily they transitioned from our homes to the streets. Bonus points went to pairs with performance perks like stretch, moisture-wicking, quick-drying, and anti-odor protection. After all was said and done, we narrowed it down to eight selections that truly stood out.

Sweatpants tend to have a looser fit throughout the leg, while joggers feature a more tailored or tapered fit. This means the legs of joggers will often touch your skin and feel a bit tight if you’ve got beefy calves.

Traditional sweatpants are often made from thicker materials like fleece and have a casual appearance, while joggers can be made from a wider range of materials, including activewear fabrics like nylon or polyester.

Joggers have ribbed cuffs at the ankles and a sportier appearance compared to the relaxed vibe of traditional sweatpants, whereas sweatpants can have either an open design or ribbed cuffs.

Sweatpants have a more relaxed and traditional look, while joggers are designed with a sporty aesthetic, often featuring modern details like zipper pockets and paneling. And because of the difference in ankle cuffs and fit, sweatpants look more casual, whereas joggers look more elevated and sleek.

Joggers are versatile for both athletic and casual wear, while sweatpants are primarily associated with casual lounging and relaxation.

Sweatpants are typically ideal for cozy indoor activities, while joggers can transition from the gym to casual outings due to their sleeker appearance.

#6769. Who Is Malin Akerman’s Husband, Jack Donnelly?

The Hunting Wives star married the British actor in 2018

Malin Akerman may stir up romantic trouble as Margo Banks, the notorious ringleader of The Hunting Wives, but off screen, her love life is much less chaotic. The actress has been happily married to fellow actor Jack Donnelly since 2018, and over the years, the two have even found ways to merge their professional and personal lives.

Akerman and Donnelly have collaborated on several projects, including four movies (and an NSFW sex scene, but more on that below). In a 2023 interview with People, the actress opened up about their relationship, crediting her husband with helping her become a better communicator.

“He’s allowed me to be vulnerable and lean into him a bit, and it’s been beautiful to be able to communicate with someone who’s your partner,” she said of their marriage.

Ahead, everything you need to know about Jack Donnelly, his career, and his marriage with Akerman.

Donnelly was born in Bournemouth, England, on October 28, 1985, and later attended the Central School of Speech and Drama in London. He made his acting debut in 2011 on the Nickelodeon series House of Anubis. Two years later, he landed a breakout lead role in the BBC’s series Atlantis, as Jason—which remains one of his best-known roles to date.

In addition to acting, Donnelly has branched out behind the camera as a television writer and director. He wrote, directed, and starred in the short comedy series The Players, which debuted in 2023.

The couple’s love story began with a family connection. Donnelly first met Akerman through her younger sister, whose boyfriend was friends with him at drama school. During a 2018 appearance on Live With Kelly and Ryan, the actress explained that she had actually known Donnelly for several years before their relationship turned romantic.

“I had known him for the past four years but didn’t see him,” she recalled. “Then all of a sudden he came back from England like a year-and-a-half ago, and I went, ‘Wow, have you always been this cute?’”

Akerman announced the couple’s engagement via Instagram in October 2017. Sharing a selfie with Donnelly and flashing her new engagement ring, she captioned the post, “This sweet and loving man has stepped into our lives and stole both our hearts,” she wrote, referring to her and her son. “I think we’re gonna keep him around for a while,” she continued with a wink.

Akerman and Donnelly tied the knot a little over a year later, on December 1, 2018, in a beach ceremony held in Tulum, Mexico. “It was all about being authentic...not adhering to somebody else’s standard of what a wedding should be,” Akerman told Brides.

“We wanted something beautiful and lush,” she continued, sharing more about their wedding venue. “When we visited Papaya Playa Project [in Tulum], we fell in love with the boho vibes and beach setting.”

In 2023, Akerman shared new photos from the beachside wedding on Instagram to celebrate their five-year anniversary.

When discussing her engagement on Live With Kelly and Ryan in 2018, Akerman also shared how excited her son, Sebastian, was about the news. The actress shares Sebastian with her ex-husband, musician Roberto Zincone, and recalled his excited reaction when she told him that she and Donnelly were getting married. “He jumped up on the couch and went, ‘Yay, we’re getting married!’” she said.

In another 2018 interview with Us Weekly, Akerman gushed about the close bond Donnelly had already formed with her then-five-year-old son. “They’re inseparable,” she said. “It makes my heart warm. I’m very glad to be bringing Jack into our lives for good.”

Over the years, the three have also made several red-carpet appearances together, offering a glimpse into their life as a family.

Akerman and Donnelly have shared the screen in four films so far: Friendsgiving, A Week in Paradise, Slayers, and Us or Them. Their first movie together was the 2020 comedy Friendsgiving, in which they played a couple—and even had to film a steamy sex scene. During a 2020 appearance on The Talk, Akerman opened up about filming the “crazy S&M scene.”

“[It was] just like being in our home,” she joked when asked what it was like filming the sex scene. She added that she was glad to have her husband as her costar. “Any chance I get to have him do a scene like that, who better than to do it with than your husband?” she said. “It’s a really, kind of, intimate, crazy scene, and thank God we know each other well enough instead of just meeting somebody on the first day and [doing] this crazy S&M scene. It was lovely to have him there.”

During a November 2026 appearance on The Drew Barrymore Show, Akerman and her The Hunting Wives costar Brittany Snow discussed Snow’s recent engagement to fiancé Hunter Moreno. When Barrymore asked if their significant others got along too, Akerman gushed, “They’re so close!”

“We call them all the Hunting Husbands,” Akerman added, sharing their nickname for all the boyfriends and husbands of the cast.

#6763. Who Is Brittany Snow’s Fiancé, Hunter Moreno?

The two began dating while Snow filmed the first season of The Hunting Wives

If you’re a fan of The Hunting Wives, chances are you’re probably invested in the on-screen affair between Brittany Snow and Malin Akerman’s characters, Sophie and Margo. But off screen, both actresses are happily in relationships—Akerman with her husband of eight years, Jack Donnelly, and Snow with new fiancé Hunter Moreno.

Snow announced the engagement news on a November 19 appearance on The Drew Barrymore Show, when Barrymore asked her directly, “Are you recently engaged?” The Hunting Wives star replied, “You know what? For the first time, I’m gonna tell you, I’m gonna tell you. Yes, we’re engaged, yeah. I haven’t said it before. It’s okay. It’s not that big of a secret. I mean, like, who cares?”

In talking about her husband-to-be, Snow revealed that the two began dating during the filming of the first season of The Hunting Wives. “He’s the best,” she grinned, adding that Akerman was one of the people to encourage the relationship early on.

Akerman, who was also a guest on the show, added, “We all met him. We got to be, like, the first ones to judge him. And the second I met him, I was like, ‘Oh, Brittany, he’s the one.’” She continued, “His smile right away. It’s just, you know, when you meet a person, and you can just feel their energy? Like, ‘This is the right energy for you.’”

The costars then playfully admitted they jokingly refer to the cast’s boyfriends and husbands as “The Hunting Husbands.”

So, who is Snow’s fiancé, Hunter Moreno? Ahead, everything to know about the creative and their relationship.

Based in Los Angeles, Moreno began his career directing and filming YouTube videos for influencers before expanding into music, directing videos for artists including Dasha, Charlie Puth, Ava Max, and Machine Gun Kelly. Along with his video credentials, he’s also photographed artists from Selena Gomez to Lizzo, Renee Rapp, and Cara Delevingne. Moreno has also shot fashion editorials and campaigns for publications and brands including Vogue and Madewell.

In 2018, Moreno founded Make Sure Your Friends Are Okay (MSYFAO), a mental health awareness organization that sells clothing and merch, with a portion of its proceeds benefiting various mental health charities.

The initiative is rooted in Moreno’s own experiences with mental health. In an Instagram post shared in May, he opened up about his struggles with depression and the personal reason he started the organization. “In 2018 I started Make Sure Your Friends Are Okay for a selfish reason I didn’t say out loud: I hoped someone would check on me while I was struggling with my own mental health issues and depression,” he wrote. He went on to encourage others to check in on the people they care about and make conversations about mental health more normalized.

Mental health advocacy is also important to Snow, who co-founded September Letters in 2020. The letter-writing platform, which has partnered with the Mental Health Coalition, aims to help end the stigma surrounding mental health.

Moreno and Snow were first publicly linked in October 2024, when they were photographed kissing while walking through New York City. During a 2026 appearance on The Drew Barrymore Show, Snow confirmed that the two began dating during the filming of The Hunting Wives’ first season, which began in 2024.

On March 28, 2026, Snow made their relationship Instagram official—though it was slyly packed into a massive carousel post. Amongst the photos from her birthday celebration in the desert were two photos of Snow and Moreno, a mirror selfie and a photo of the two posing with her dog.

Though Moreno’s Instagram is mostly dedicated to his professional work, in September, he uploaded a carousel of photos taken of Snow while traveling from Italy to France.

Rumors of an engagement first sparked between Snow and Moreno in November 2025, when a video shared by DeuxMoi showed Claire Danes chatting with Snow on a red carpet and seemingly congratulating her on her engagement. “You’re engaged?!” Danes exclaimed during the exchange. Nearly a year later, Snow finally confirmed the news during an appearance on The Drew Barrymore Show.

“You know what? For the first time, I’m gonna tell you, I’m gonna tell you. Yes, we’re engaged, yeah,” Snow said when Barrymore asked her directly. “I haven’t said it before. It’s okay. It’s not that big of a secret. I mean, like, who cares?”

While gushing about her fiancé alongside Barrymore and her Hunting Wives costar Akerman, Snow called Moreno “the best.” She also revealed that Akerman played a role in their love story, encouraging Snow to pursue the relationship early on.

BLACK FRIDAY IS officially here, and Cole Haan is rolling out some pretty sweet deals with markdowns up to 68 percent off select styles on the brand’s website and Amazon. This is the time of year when I get dressed up the most—layering up for the cold office commutes and getting ready for holiday gatherings, so I’m going to need comfortable dress shoes to help me get through it all. That’s why Cole Haan has become one of my go-to destinations for just that. The brand has a knack for combining classic, polished styles with the level of comfort you’d expect from your favorite sneakers. Everything from dressy oxfords to every day dress sneakers that can easily pull double duty is seeing a price drop. It’s not everyday these shoes receive a markdown, so I wouldn’t bank on your size still being available as the hours go on.

Cole Haan ØriginalGrand Horizon Wingtip Oxfords

Cole Haan 2.ZERØGRAND Laser Wingtip Oxfords

Cole Haan GrandPrø SkyWeave Low-Profile Sneakers

One of the biggest discounts is the 2.ZERØGRAND Laser Wingtip Oxford, marked down 58 percent. It’s like a mashup of an athletic sneaker and a traditional dress shoe. The nubuck and polyester upper features a laser-cut wingtip pattern that puts a modern twist on classic broguing, while the lightweight sole keeps things from feeling too stiff or clunky. I’ve found that the Grandfoam—the brand’s proprietary cushioning—has a slightly squishy feel underfoot, but in a good way. I always throw this sneaker on for our photo shoot days that tend to go over eight hours.

Now, if you’re looking for something a little more traditional, the Harmon Grand Cap Toe Oxford (30 percent off) is the way to go. Unlike the other two styles, this lace-up model leans more into classic dress-shoe territory, with a sleek cap-toe design that feels right at home with tailored trousers or a suit. I’d pull this out for a holiday party or any occasion that calls for looking a little more buttoned-up.

I’ve covered a number of Black Friday sales, but this is Cole Haan’s best one yet. I’m already seeing sizes fly off the shelves, so there’s no telling what deals will be left by the end of the day. If you’ve been eyeing a new pair of dress shoes or dress sneakers, I’d take advantage of these markdowns ASAP.

#6758. Hailey Bieber Does Island Style the Chanel Way

She and the Jenners gave Bahamas dressing the It Girl update

Hailey Bieber turned 30 on November 22, but the birthday girl has been celebrating for weeks. From a new navy-blue Rhode drop to a pre-birthday birthday party (for which she wore a vintage python bustier top from Dolce & Gabbana’s Spring 2005 collection), she’s been ushering in the new decade with work milestones and time with friends and family. And before the end of the month, she finished off the festivities with a trip to the Bahamas.

Joined by her husband, Justin, along with a handful of girlfriends—Kendall and Kylie Jenner, Camila Morrone, Justine Skye, and more—the beauty founder spent her birthday weekend on island time. And, of course, with an island style to match.

In photos shared to her Instagram, Bieber is seen in everything from a gold metallic bikini to a raffia cowboy hat and some beachy party fits.

Over the itty-bitty two-piece, the star layered a sheer cover-up from the Dolce & Gabbana Spring 1997 runway. A warm-weather take on the fur-trimmed silk-brocade robe favored this fall, the long leopard-print piece featured deep-brown fur lapels, long sleeves, and a breezy, billowing body. She paired the look with sunglasses and a flap bag from Matthieu Blazy’s Resort 2026 collection for Chanel. The on-theme accessory was crafted with a braided raffia body and “fluffy” yellow straw trim.

Later, Bieber changed into straight-off-the-runway pieces from the Roberto Cavalli Spring 2027 show. On top, she wore a forest-green top with a lace-up front and flared trumpet sleeves, and for bottoms, she went with low-rise zebra-print trousers.

As for her friends, the Jenners were photographed in beachy ensembles of their own: Kendall in a bikini and mustard-yellow sarong, and Kylie in a brown crochet cover-up dress.
